Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any quantum_mechanics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 921. The projective resolution has length at most 330 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_921(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 921. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^330(S, F) is trivial. QED.
